Golgi apparatus membrane** — Golgi glycosyltransferases have different domain architectures.<br>- **D. The P-loop motifs (Walker A) in our sequence are NOT typical of glycosidases. Glycosidases use different catalytic mechanisms (retaining: two carboxylates as nucleophile and acid/base; inverting: one carboxylate as base, water as nucleophile). They don&#x27;t typically have P-loop motifs.

So the P-loop motifs strongly argue against option B (and D, which is also a glycosidase reaction).

This leaves us with A (phosphatase) or C (kinase).

The GDK motif argues for phosphatase (A) over kinase (C).

Final answer: A.

Kinases typically have:

Phosphatases (P-type, PPM):

- Often smaller proteins
